Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the ecology framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 510. The resulting operator achieves bound O(649/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to ecology theory, we decompose the problem into 510 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
